- **onpage**: Audit and fix the on-page elements of one URL: title element, meta description, H1 and heading outline, canonical, robots directives, internal links, image alt text, structured data and content depth. Reads what a crawler sees with the page, schema and cite commands, explains why Google may rewrite a title link (six causes from Google's own documentation) and what to change first. Use when the user says 'on-page SEO', 'optimize this page', 'audit this one page', 'why did Google change my title', 'page checklist', 'check my H1', 'is this page ready to publish'. - **onpage-audit**: Run a technical on-page audit of a set of pages on a Search Console property with the GSC Wizard MCP tools: pick the URLs from the user's list, the top pages by clicks, or a sitemap, crawl them in batches with audit_onpage_seo (indexability, canonical, titles and meta, headings, links, images, structured data), optionally confirm a canonical mismatch with a quota-aware URL inspection, then report issues grouped by severity with affected URLs and fixes. Use when the user says "audit these pages", "check my on-page SEO", "technical check of my top pages", "are my titles and meta descriptions ok… ([jbobbink/seo-gsc-wizard](https://github.com/jbobbink/seo-gsc-wizard/tree/HEAD/skills/onpage-audit) · ★1)
- **onpage-audit**: Runs the onpage-seo-auditor agent over the whole site (blogs, services, industries, locations, case studies) against the Semrush-based on-page checklist, applies safe fixes, and coaches the writer and QA agents. - **onpage-content-generation**: Redaction de contenu SEO on-page - title tags, meta descriptions, alt text, contenu sections par secteur ([ikastner/skills-larockette](https://github.com/ikastner/skills-larockette/tree/HEAD/40-qa-performance/onpage-content-generation))
- **onpage-optimization**: Validates heading hierarchy, keyword density and placement, and internal/external link structure. Use when auditing on-page SEO factors for an HTML page. - **onpage-optimizer**: Use to audit and optimize a single page's on-page SEO. Detects whether it is a blog post or a service/product page and applies the matching checklist, then rewrites the title tag, meta description, headings and schema. Step 3 of the SAU sequence. Also use for on-page audits, title and meta rewrites, schema fixes, or when asked why a page isn't ranking. ([galileo-studio-dev/skills](https://github.com/galileo-studio-dev/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/sau/skills/onpage-optimizer))
- **onpage-seo**: On-page SEO for any website, by OnPage Agent. Audits a live site from one URL (titles, meta descriptions, headings, canonicals, noindex, broken links, redirects, sitemap, robots.txt, structured data, mobile viewport, JavaScript-only pages, AI crawler access, llms.txt, HTTPS and security headers), ranks what to fix first, and then fixes it: directly in the codebase for Next.js, Astro, React/Vite (Lovable, Bolt, v0), Nuxt, SvelteKit and plain HTML, or with exact steps for WordPress, Webflow, Wix, Shopify and Squarespace. Also sets up Google Search Console, Google Analytics 4 and Bing Webmaster… ([OnPage-Agent/onpage-seo](https://github.com/OnPage-Agent/onpage-seo/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **onpar**: Work out which open-weight LLM can run on a machine, at what quantization, context and concurrency; which serving runtime to use; and whether an open model is actually good enough to replace a closed one on someone's own traffic. - **onping-audit-pull**: Pull the OnPing audit trail via POST /log/audit2/query — who changed a setpoint, alarm, site, or dashboard, and when. Read-only. Use when a question is about history and attribution rather than current state. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-audit-pull))
- **onping-audit-recover**: Recover prior state for any audited OnPing model from the audit database in postgres. Read-only, SELECT only. Reaches the models POST /log/audit2/query cannot see, because the audit write path is generic while the read path is an enumerated list. Emits columns as stored and deliberately does not decode per-model encodings. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-audit-recover))
- **onping-create-singlewell-manual**: Create one OnPing singlewell-manual location on a Lumberjack through POST /singlewellmanual/location/add. MUTATING — previews by default, posts only with --yes, refuses a group you do not own, a site outside the company, or a duplicate name at the site, and reads every sent field back with /singlewellmanual/get/loc. Add parameters afterwards with onping-import-singlewell-manual. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-create-singlewell-manual))
- **onping-ctable-audit-export**: Pull one custom_table_widget_audit row and decode it into the JSON shape POST /content/ctable/json accepts. Read-only, SELECT only. Handles the four encodings that differ between the audit table and the API, including the S-prefix that applies to exactly two fields. Emits a bare widget by default so it cannot be posted by accident. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-ctable-audit-export))
- **onping-ctable-audit-history**: List the save history of an OnPing custom-table widget from the audit database — when, who, action, and payload size per version. Read-only, SELECT only. Reaches history that POST /log/audit2/query cannot see at all, and flags the size step that means a table was overwritten rather than edited. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-ctable-audit-history))
- **onping-ctable-export**: Export an OnPing custom-table widget as JSON via GET /content/ctable/json. Read-only. The backup half of the custom-table restore round trip, and the first step before any onping-ctable-import. Reports the payload size against the 8 MiB cap that the import route enforces. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-ctable-export))
- **onping-ctable-import**: Write a CustomTableWidget to OnPing via POST /content/ctable/json — a full-document mongo repsert that preserves the caller's ObjectId. MUTATING, requires --yes. Restores a widget from onping-ctable-export or onping-ctable-audit-export. Refuses the four payloads that quietly ruin a widget, and verifies the write by readback. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-ctable-import))
- **onping-datatable-cell-set**: Adjust the PIDs in an OnPing Lumberjack data table — set one cell (--row + --column to --pid/--vpid/--text/--clear) or rewrite every cell pointing at a PID or VPID (--replace-pid OLD NEW) — through POST /datatable/&lt;LJ&gt;/source/content/update. MUTATING — previews by default, posts only with --yes, backs up the edited rows, and verifies the read-back. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-datatable-cell-set))
- **onping-datatable-column-add**: Add an empty column (name + selector, optional position) to an OnPing Lumberjack data table through POST /datatable/&lt;LJ&gt;/source/metadata/update. MUTATING — previews by default, posts only with --yes, backs up the TableSource first, and verifies the read-back. Fill the new cells with onping-datatable-cell-set. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-datatable-column-add))
- **onping-datatable-column-delete**: Delete a column from an OnPing Lumberjack data table (DROP COLUMN — its cells are destroyed) through POST /datatable/&lt;LJ&gt;/source/metadata/update. MUTATING — previews the data loss by default, posts only with --yes, backs up the TableSource AND every row's pointers first, and verifies the read-back. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-datatable-column-delete))
- **onping-datatable-fetch**: Read an OnPing Lumberjack data table's rows with both the rendered value and the stored PID/VPID pointer of every cell; print one row as a unit (--row), or find every cell pointing at a PID or VPID (--pid / --vpid). Read-only. Use to see which PIDs a data-table row names before building or checking a log table. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-datatable-fetch))
- **onping-datatable-get**: Read one OnPing Lumberjack data table's TableSource — every column's position, UUID, selector, hidden flag, and name, plus pagination, order, and stored filters — or the raw JSON (--json / --output, also the backup format). Read-only. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-datatable-get))
- **onping-datatable-list**: List OnPing Lumberjack data tables (the /datatable sources) on one LJ or every LJ — LJ, UUID, column count, group, name — and find every table with a cell pointing at a PID or VPID (--references-pid / --references-vpid). Read-only. Start here to get a data table's UUID; not the ctable widget. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-datatable-list))
- **onping-delete-singlewell-manual**: Delete one OnPing singlewell-manual location through POST /singlewellmanual/delete. MUTATING, no undo — previews by default, posts only with --yes, refuses a group you do not own or a location that still has parameters, backs up the full config first, and decides success from a read-back, never from the HTTP status or body (servers before the 2026.11.5.1 fix answered HTTP 200 for every outcome). Reports the records the server leaves behind. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-delete-singlewell-manual))
- **onping-doc-category**: List, create, update, and trash sidebar categories on the OnPing customer-facing documentation site (Payload CMS) via the three *Categories MCP tools and moveToTrash. --list is read-only; create, update, and delete (a soft delete — move to trash) are MUTATING and require --yes, with a --confirm-count guard on any --where bulk mutation. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-doc-category))
- **onping-doc-create**: Create a document on the OnPing customer-facing documentation site (Payload CMS) via the createDocs MCP tool. MUTATING — requires --yes, and prints the exact tool call without it. Creates an empty page; publish its body with onping-doc-write. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-doc-create))
- **onping-doc-delete**: Move documents to the trash on the OnPing customer-facing documentation site (Payload CMS) via the moveToTrash MCP tool — a soft delete, the only delete the server offers over MCP. MUTATING — a single-id trash needs --yes, and a --where bulk trash needs --yes AND a --confirm-count matching the resolved count. Only a human can restore, from the admin panel; back up with onping-doc-get --json first. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-doc-delete))
- **onping-doc-get**: Read one document from the OnPing customer-facing documentation site (Payload CMS) by id or slug, and report its Lexical content shape. Read-only. Use to inspect or back up a document before editing it with onping-doc-write or removing it with onping-doc-delete. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-doc-get))
- **onping-doc-list**: List and filter documents on the OnPing customer-facing documentation site (Payload CMS) via the findDocs and getDocsByCategory MCP tools. Read-only. Use to discover document ids, slugs, and categories before reading with onping-doc-get or publishing with onping-doc-write. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-doc-list))
- **onping-doc-write**: Publish markdown to a page on the OnPing customer-facing documentation site (Payload CMS) via updateDocWithMarkdown, under a controlled-language standard and the three canonical OnPing personas. MUTATING — requires --yes. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-doc-write))
- **onping-driver-resolve**: Resolve OnPing location refIds to their driver slug (read-only). Use to find which onping-update-&lt;driver&gt; / onping-add-&lt;driver&gt; skill applies to a bare location refId. Calls /singlewellextras/lister; does NOT mutate. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-driver-resolve))
- **onping-event-table-export**: Export an OnPing event table as Dhall via GET /event/table/export/&lt;file&gt;?eventTableUUID=&lt;uuid&gt;. Read-only. Backs up a table, or gives a starting point for the event-table-import template. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-event-table-export))
- **onping-event-table-fetch**: Fetch the rows an OnPing event table renders at a given time via POST /event/table/fetch. Read-only. Confirms whether a table renders, and names the dead PID when one stops it. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-event-table-fetch))
- **onping-event-table-get**: Read an OnPing event table's configuration as JSON via POST /event/table/config. Read-only. Shows which PID or VPID each column reads (--bindings) and answers whether a given PID is bound (--pid). ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-event-table-get))
- **onping-event-table-list**: List OnPing event tables from dashboard JSON — dashboard, panel, widget title, and UUID — and find every table that binds a given PID (--references-pid). Read-only. Use to get from a widget title to an event-table UUID, or to audit which tables read a deleted PID. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-event-table-list))
- **onping-export-control-logix**: Download the OnPing control-logix driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-control-logix))
- **onping-export-dnp3**: Download the OnPing dnp3 driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-dnp3))
- **onping-export-lumberjack-remote**: Download the OnPing lumberjack-remote driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-lumberjack-remote))
- **onping-export-micrologix**: Download the OnPing micrologix driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-micrologix))
- **onping-export-modbus-flexible**: Download the OnPing modbus-flexible driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-modbus-flexible))
- **onping-export-mqtt-json**: Download the OnPing mqtt-json driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-mqtt-json))
- **onping-export-opc-ua**: Download the OnPing opc-ua driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-opc-ua))
- **onping-export-roc-tlp**: Download the OnPing roc-tlp driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-roc-tlp))
- **onping-export-singlewell-manual**: Download the OnPing singlewell-manual driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-singlewell-manual))
- **onping-export-sparkplug-bridge**: Download the OnPing sparkplug-bridge driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-sparkplug-bridge))
- **onping-export-total-flow**: Download the OnPing total-flow driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-total-flow))
- **onping-export-wellpilot**: Download the OnPing wellpilot driver tag export as XLSX. Performs an authenticated GET and writes the file locally. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-export-wellpilot))
- **onping-generic-swd-get**: Show one generic-SWD offload kiosk's configuration (name, lanes with their read PID, mask script, write PID and waiting-to-start value, and the status map), optionally its live lane states and recent tickets, and save it as a backup file that onping-generic-swd-update can restore. Read-only. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-generic-swd-get))
- **onping-generic-swd-list**: List the generic-SWD offload kiosks (including Ticket Permissive kiosks) in the OnPing groups you own or belong to, with each kiosk's UUID, name, group and whether you own that group. Read-only. Use it to find the kiosk UUID that onping-generic-swd-get and onping-generic-swd-update need. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-generic-swd-list))
- **onping-generic-swd-update**: Change one generic-SWD offload kiosk's configuration (including Ticket Permissive kiosks) - rename it, set, add or delete a lane (read PID, mask script, write PID, waiting-to-start value), set or delete a status-map entry, or restore a whole configuration from a backup. MUTATING with `--yes` (preview by default, `--dry-run` wins). Needs ownership of the kiosk's group, and every new or changed lane PID must pass OnPing's PID-write ownership rule. Each write is validated, backed up, checked for concurrent edits, sent once and confirmed by read-back and a live lane read. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-generic-swd-update))
- **onping-hmi-delete**: Delete an OnPing HMI dashboard by UUID via DELETE /hmi/delete/{uuid}. MUTATING, requires --yes. Soft delete (sets dashDeleted=true). Back up first with onping-hmi-export. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-hmi-delete))
- **onping-hmi-export**: Export a full OnPing HMI dashboard to a Dhall file via GET /hmi/export/{uuid}. Read-only. Backs up an HMI (layout, components, settings, alerts) for restore or cloning with onping-hmi-import. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-hmi-export))
- **onping-hmi-export-data**: Export an OnPing HMI's data-binding mappings to a Dhall file via GET /hmi/export-data/{uuid}. Read-only. Exports only the OnPing-parameter bindings ([DataImport]), not the layout — re-point an HMI's data with onping-hmi-import-data. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-hmi-export-data))
- **onping-hmi-import**: Import a full OnPing HMI dashboard from an exported Dhall file via POST /hmi/parse then POST /hmi/upsert. MUTATING, requires --yes. Overwrites the HMI whose dashId is in the file, or creates a copy with --new. Restore an onping-hmi-export backup or clone an HMI. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-hmi-import))
- **onping-hmi-import-data**: Import data-binding mappings into an existing OnPing HMI from a Dhall file via POST /hmi/import-data/{uuid}. MUTATING, requires --yes. Re-points which OnPing parameters an HMI reads ([DataImport]) without changing layout. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-hmi-import-data))
- **onping-hmi-list**: List the OnPing HMIs available to the user via GET /hmi/list. Read-only. Returns each HMI's name, UUID, dashboard, and panel — the discovery entry point for finding UUIDs to export, back up, or delete. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-hmi-list))
- **onping-import-modbus-flexible**: Import an edited OnPing modbus-flexible tag spreadsheet into one location via POST /modbus/flexible/import/params (multipart f1=file, f2=location int). MUTATES OnPing with --yes. Matches rows to tags by Index (the server ignores the PID column when it rebuilds tags), refuses duplicate Index, duplicate PID and silent PID changes, and reads the result back by re-export. The flow is export → edit/append → re-import. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-import-modbus-flexible))
- **onping-import-mqtt-json**: Upload an OnPing mqtt-json parameter spreadsheet to POST /mqtt/json/param/import (multipart form; File + Location). MUTATES OnPing with --yes. Bulk UPDATE, not authoring — the sheet must contain every existing PID, so the flow is export → edit → re-import. SKILL.md documents the full parameter/JQ format. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-import-mqtt-json))
- **onping-import-singlewell-manual**: Upload an OnPing singlewell-manual parameter spreadsheet to POST /v2/singlewellmanual/import/params (multipart form; f1=file + f2=location int). MUTATES OnPing with --yes. CREATES and/or UPDATES manual parameters — a blank Pid row creates, a populated Pid row updates. Cannot delete, so the flow is export → edit/append → re-import. SKILL.md documents the 5-column format and value-type tags. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-import-singlewell-manual))
- **onping-line-graph**: Build OnPing line graph / chart widgets in Dhall ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-line-graph))
- **onping-line-graph-create**: Mint a fresh empty OnPing line-graph widget via POST /content/widgets/line-graph/config. Returns a new `o…` LineGraphWidgetId. MUTATING, requires --yes. The resulting widget is orphaned (not attached to a dashboard). Use with onping-line-graph-import to populate; there is no delete route for cleanup. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-line-graph-create))
- **onping-line-graph-export**: Export an OnPing line-graph widget to a Dhall file via GET /content/widgets/line-graph/export/{id}. Read-only. Backs up a full widget (title, yAxes, eventParameters, all display config) for restore or cloning with onping-line-graph-import. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-line-graph-export))
- **onping-line-graph-export-data**: Export an OnPing line-graph widget's pid bindings as a Dhall [Field] list via GET /content/widgets/line-graph/export-data-only/{id}. Read-only. Distinct shape from onping-line-graph-export — a bare list of from→to pid mappings; edit and re-import via onping-line-graph-import-data to remap pids. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-line-graph-export-data))
- **onping-line-graph-get**: Read an OnPing line-graph widget as JSON via GET /content/widgets/line-graph/widget/{id}. Read-only. Inspects a widget's title, timePeriod, yAxes, eventParameters, and pids without a Dhall round-trip. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-line-graph-get))
- **onping-line-graph-import**: Import a full OnPing line-graph widget from a Dhall / JSON / YAML file via POST /content/widgets/line-graph/import/{id}. MUTATING, requires --yes. Overwrites the target widget in place; `--new` mints a fresh id and imports into it (two-step). --dry-run runs locally (no /parse endpoint) and surfaces the pid list the server will permission-check. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-line-graph-import))
- **onping-line-graph-import-data**: Remap pid bindings on an OnPing line-graph widget via POST /content/widgets/line-graph/import-data-only/{id}. Consumes a Dhall [Field] list of from→to OnpingKey mappings. MUTATING, requires --yes. Rewrites pids only — leaves title / colors / axis descriptions / event icons untouched. Distinct shape from onping-line-graph-import. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-line-graph-import-data))
- **onping-locations**: Fetch OnPing locations for given sites. Use after fetching sites (onping-sites) to list the locations belonging to those sites using their refId values. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-locations))
- **onping-login**: Authenticate with OnPing via OAuth2 and get an access token. Use when needing to access OnPing APIs or when the user mentions OnPing login, authentication, or session. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-login))
- **onping-logtable-column-add**: Add a column recording a PID or VPID (with a selector, default Result, and optional position) to an OnPing log table, as a new schema version through POST /logtable/&lt;LJ&gt;/source/schema/upgrade. MUTATING — previews by default, posts only with --yes, backs up the LogTableSource, verifies the read-back. The new version starts with no rows. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-column-add))
- **onping-logtable-column-delete**: Remove a column (by position, UUID, name, or the PID/VPID it records) from an OnPing log table, as a new schema version through POST /logtable/&lt;LJ&gt;/source/schema/upgrade. Refuses the event column. MUTATING — previews by default, posts only with --yes, backs up the LogTableSource, verifies the read-back. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-column-delete))
- **onping-logtable-column-set**: Repoint an OnPing log-table column to another PID or VPID, change its selector, and/or rename it — keeping its UUID — as a new schema version through POST /logtable/&lt;LJ&gt;/source/schema/upgrade. Repointing the event column moves the trigger (PID only). MUTATING — previews by default, posts only with --yes, backs up the LogTableSource, verifies the read-back. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-column-set))
- **onping-logtable-create**: Create an OnPing log table on a Lumberjack with all its columns in one step — POST source/create, then one version-1 schema/upgrade holding the LastUpdateDateTime event column keyed by the trigger PID (--event-pid) plus every column given with --column NAME=SOURCE[:TAG], or copied from an onping-logtable-export XLSX with --from. MUTATING — checks the columns before any request, previews by default, posts only with --yes, verifies the read-back, and names any orphaned version-0 source. No dashboard is needed. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-create))
- **onping-logtable-delete**: Delete an OnPing log table (soft delete — it stops recording and leaves the list; no undelete route) through POST /logtable/&lt;LJ&gt;/source/delete. MUTATING — previews the table and its row count by default, posts only with --yes, backs up the full LogTableSource first, and verifies it is gone from the list. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-delete))
- **onping-logtable-export**: Download a log-table source's XLSX (multi-row-header variant) from POST /logtable/#LJSerial/source/export/query/multiple-rows-header/#String. Resolves the source record via GET /logtable/#LJSerial/source/list, then POSTs the required (LogTableSource, Int) tuple as the JSON body. Read-only. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-export))
- **onping-logtable-fetch**: Read an OnPing log table's recorded rows newest first, with the row count and the newest event time and its age — "is it still recording?" — optionally between --since/--until (UTC, inclusive, minute precision) or from an older schema --version. Read-only. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-fetch))
- **onping-logtable-get**: Read one OnPing log table's LogTableSource — the current (or --version N) schema's columns with position, UUID, PID/VPID key, selector, event marker, hidden flag, and name; the version list; and schema problems — or the raw JSON (--json / --output). Read-only. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-get))
- **onping-logtable-import**: Upload a multiple-rows-header log-table XLSX to POST /logtable/#LJSerial/source/import/template/multiple-rows-header (multipart form; f1=file + f2=TableId UUID). MUTATES OnPing with --yes. Requires an existing log-table source. Accepts `source: &lt;n&gt;` (PID) and `source: &lt;n&gt; VPID`; rejects anything else (`source: TODO` placeholders included) locally to pre-empt the server's `Invalid PID or VPID format for` error. To create a source use onping-logtable-create; to edit one column use onping-logtable-column-add / -column-delete / -column-set. SKILL.md documents the 4-row `event`/`name:`/`tag:`/`sou… ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-import))
- **onping-logtable-list**: List OnPing log tables on one LJ or every LJ — LJ, UUID, schema version, column count, event (trigger) PID, name — and find every log table with a column recording a PID or VPID (--references-pid / --references-vpid). Read-only. Start here to get a log table's UUID or to check whether a data-table row's PIDs are already logged. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-logtable-list))
- **onping-mass-write**: Build a validated OnPing mass-write CSV from timestamp+value points. Builds the file only — does NOT upload. The user takes the resulting CSV to the OnPing UI mass-import dialog, which writes to the cloud server only (not local Lumberjack edge servers). ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mass-write))
- **onping-massdelete**: Delete OnPing historian samples by posting a JSON array of {deletePid, deleteDate} to POST /massdelete/execute. Input is the event-report CSV shape with populated cells rewritten to `1.0`. MUTATES OnPing with --yes; --dry-run previews locally. Chunks large payloads. SKILL.md documents the CSV shape and the UTC-timestamp policy. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-massdelete))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-artifacts-export**: Download one Lumberjack's mqtt-json-integrator created-object record as XLSX via GET /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/artifacts/export/{filename}, or losslessly as JSON via GET .../artifacts. Read-only. This is the integrator's record of what it BELIEVES it created — one-way data flow, never verified against OnPing, values frozen at rule-execution time. The XLSX round-trip is LOSSY (PID timestamps dropped, unknown values collapse to 0.0), so use --json to back up. Its live function is create-suppression, not just audit. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-artifacts-export))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-artifacts-import**: Upload an artifacts XLSX to POST /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/artifacts/import (multipart form; f1 = the file). MUTATES the integrator's record with --yes but CREATES NOTHING in OnPing or the mqtt-json driver — the route that creates real objects is POST .../artifacts (onping-mqtt-integrator-create). Its actual purpose is SUPPRESSION: anything recorded as created is skipped by the create pipeline. A repsert, not a replace — absent rows are not deleted. Column 3 (Location ID Ref) is trusted verbatim and never verified. The export that produces this sheet is lossy, so back up with --json firs… ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-artifacts-import))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-blacklist**: Read or edit the mqtt-json-integrator blacklist for one Lumberjack via GET/POST /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/blacklist — the list of locations and PIDs that must never be created, filtered server-side in step one of the create pipeline so they are skipped even with --all. MUTATES with --yes. Blacklisting BLOCKS CREATION but does NOT delete anything already created. The POST takes incremental add/remove ops, not a whole list, so concurrent editors do not clobber each other. PIDs need three values (location key, topic, value selector) because PidUniqueIdentifier is a record, not a scalar. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-blacklist))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-config**: Read or update one Lumberjack's mqtt-json-integrator MQTT config (broker, topic, auto-execute flag, unprocessed-queue cap) via GET/POST /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/mqtt/config. Read-modify-write, because the handler consumes a whole MqttConfig and a partial body would drop fields. MUTATES OnPing only with --yes; refuses to change the Company/Site/Group identity fields that decide where generated objects land. Keyed by LJSerial, not location refId. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-config))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-create**: Create real OnPing locations and mqtt-json driver parameters from the integrator's uncreated candidates via POST /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/artifacts. MUTATES OnPing with --yes and there is NO UNDO. HTTP 200 does not mean success — the CreateArtifactsReport carries createLocationErrors/createPidErrors arrays and a run where everything failed still returns 200, so this skill exits non-zero when either is non-empty. Requires --location-url (the LJ's lumberjackUrl, from lj-profile) and a port defaulting to 2000 as the OnPing UI hardcodes. Not to be confused with .../artifacts/import, which c… ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-create))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-delete**: Delete mqtt-json-integrator uncreated candidates, created-object records, or the unprocessed queue for one Lumberjack via POST .../uncreated/delete, .../stored/delete, or .../unprocessed/json/objects/delete. MUTATES with --yes, and a full wipe additionally requires --all. All three are integrator-local and never delete OnPing or driver objects — but --target stored is dangerous: the real objects keep existing while orphaned, and forgetting the record UN-SUPPRESSES creation so a later run can make DUPLICATES. --target uncreated is nearly harmless since the next rule execution regenerates the c… ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-delete))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-reports**: Read mqtt-json-integrator rule-execution reports for one Lumberjack via GET /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/execute/rules/report — the ONLY place execution errors and match counts surface, since the execute route itself returns an empty envelope. --execute --yes runs all stored rules against all stored unprocessed data (blocking, serialized server-side, creates nothing in OnPing) and then prints the resulting report. --delete-all --yes clears the whole history; no per-report delete exists. The counts report NEW objects only, so 0/0 means "nothing new", not necessarily "nothing matched". ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-reports))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-rule-parse**: Validate mqtt-json-integrator generation-rule expressions via POST /mqtt/json/integrator/rule/parse (full grammar) or /jq/rule/parse (JQ-only). Read-only, stateless, and NOT scoped to a Lumberjack — no serial needed. Use before onping-mqtt-integrator-rules-import, whose only other failure signal is a 400 megaparsec dump. A parse failure arrives as HTTP 200 with a FailedToParseRules tag, so status alone is not a verdict; this skill exits non-zero instead. SKILL.md documents the rule grammar (static text, {topic|s#pat#rep#} sed, {jq}, template fields). ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-rule-parse))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-rules-export**: Download one Lumberjack's mqtt-json-integrator generation rules as XLSX via GET /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/rules/export/{filename}, or as JSON via GET .../generation/rules. Read-only. The XLSX is the round-trip format for onping-mqtt-integrator-rules-import, which REPLACES ALL RULES — so exporting first is mandatory, not optional. Only the JSON carries the rule identifiers; the sheet omits them. --summary shows how the import will group rows into location blocks, and warns when a location's rows are non-contiguous. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-rules-export))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-rules-import**: Upload a generation-rule XLSX to POST /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/rules/import (multipart form; f1 = the file). MUTATES OnPing with --yes. DESTRUCTIVE — this REPLACES THE ENTIRE RULE SET, so any rule missing from the sheet is deleted, and every surviving rule is renumbered because identifiers are derived from row order. The skill fetches the live rules first and reports exactly what would be added, kept, and DROPPED before writing. Rules are grouped by CONSECUTIVE rows sharing columns 1-3; non-adjacent blocks for one location silently become two rules. SKILL.md documents the 12-column form… ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-rules-import))
- **onping-mqtt-integrator-unprocessed**: Inspect the mqtt-json-integrator unprocessed queue for one Lumberjack via GET /mqtt/json/integrator/{serial}/unprocessed/data — the raw MQTT topic/message pairs collected, plus the uncreated locations and PIDs the rules generated from them. Read-only by default; --clear --yes drops ALL pairs unconditionally (no selection possible, no undo, no import counterpart). The queue does NOT evict when capped — it ignores new messages, which is the usual reason a healthy broker looks silent. Entries are unique per (topic, message) pair, not per topic. ([plow-technologies/onping-skills](https://github.com/plow-technologies/onping-skills/tree/HEAD/onping/skills/onping-mqtt-integrator-unprocessed))
- **onping-pak-transport-link**: List, show, export, create, edit and delete the PAK transport Ticket Permissive link between a PAK facility and a Lumberjack, with its bay-to-lane mappings (`--map BAY=LANE`, repeatable). MUTATING with `--yes` (preview by default, `--dry-run` wins). Every write passes a local preflight that mirrors the cloud app's rejections and an ownership check on the stored record, saves a backup first, is sent once, and is read back.
